#.APLGUI.APLGUI.TableEdit.CreateParms — 0%↟↑
p←CreateParms [1] :Access Public Shared [2] ⍝ Returns a parameter namespace with default settings for being feed to `Run` or `Create`. [3] ⍝ | Parameter name |Comment| [4] ⍝ | -------------------|-------| [5] ⍝ | `askNewRowTitle` | Defaults to `1` but only in case row titles are defined: in case a new row is created the user will be asked for a row title then.| [6] ⍝ | `autoExpand` | Defaults to `1`. This allows the user to add rows. Suppress this by setting this to `0`.| [7] ⍝ | `addToCaption` | Defaults to "Table Editor" but only if "caption" is not set. Otherwise there is no default.| [8] ⍝ | `caption` | No default. Overwrite the global "caption" when specified. | [9] ⍝ | `cellSelect` | No default. Use this to select a number of rows and columns. Will be ignored if "settings" contains "cellSelect". | [10] ⍝ | `cellWidths` | No default. The width of the columns is calculated based on the data. Instead you can specify the widths of the columns here. Specify `¯1` if you the default size for a column. Will be ignored if "settings" contains "cellWidths". | [11] ⍝ | `centerIn` | No default. You can specify a form the form shall be centered within. | [12] ⍝ | `copy` | `1` | [13] ⍝ | `ctrl_Pos1_End` | Defaults to "LikeExcel". You can specify an empty vector instead.| [14] ⍝ | `colTitleFCol` | `0` | [15] ⍝ | `colTitles` | No defaults. If specified this must be a vector of character vectors with a length mathing the number of columns.| [16] ⍝ | `colTitleDepth` | `⍬` | [17] ⍝ | `comments` | No default. Instead this can be a vector with comment definitions. Every item must then be a vector of length 3 with (row, column, char vector). `⋄` is treated as separator within the character vectors.| [18] ⍝ | `comment_Cmds` | Defaults to `0`. Use this to enable (`1`) or disable (`0`) all comment-related menu items ("Add", "Delete", "Edit") in one go.| [19] ⍝ | `comment_Add` | Defaults to `¯1`. Use this to disable (`0`) or enable (```) the "Add comment" menu item.| [20] ⍝ | `comment.Edit` | Defaults to `¯1`. Use this to disable (`0`) or enable (```) the "Edit comment" menu item.| [21] ⍝ | `comment_delete` | Defaults to `¯1`. Use this to disable (`0`) or enable (```) the "Delete comment" menu item.| [22] ⍝ | `confirmQuit` | Defaults to `1`. The user will be asked whether she wants to save her changes. Suppress this prompt with 0.| [23] ⍝ | `contextMenu` | Default to a matrix: `0 2⍴⊂''`. Add commands: (caption fully-qualified-callback-fns). Note that there is a default context menu; additional commands will be appended.| [24] ⍝ | `curCell` | Defines the cell that has initially the focus. Defaults to `(0 0)`. | [25] ⍝ | `cut` | Defaults to `1`. This means that the menu "Cut" item is active.| [26] ⍝ | `data` | Default is `⍬`. You may assign a matrix to it with the same number of rows as the table. This is copied onto `n.∆data`. `Table` then assures that `n.∆data` stays in line with the table when the user performs operations like "move row(s)", "delete row" or "insert row".| [27] ⍝ | `dataTypes` | Defaults to `''`. Can be a vector of data types. The length must match the number of columns. Populated with `##.Enums.TableDataTypes`.| [28] ⍝ | `debug` | Defaults to `0`. `1` Enables debug mode. See also `testMode`.| ⍝TODO⍝ [29] ⍝ | `cut` | Defaults to `1`. This means that the menu "Cut" item is active.| [30] ⍝ | `editMenu` | Defaults to `1`. Makes the "Edit" menu available. Suppress this with a `0`.| [31] ⍝ | `H_scroll` | Defaults to `¯3`: the horizontal scroll bar is always visible.| [32] ⍝ | `icon` | Defaults to `''`. | ⍝TODO⍝ [33] ⍝ | `inactiveColor` | Defaults to `230 230 230`. This color is used for read-only columns.| [34] ⍝ | `index` | First cell in the top/left corner. Defaults to `0 0`. | [35] ⍝ | `lists` | Defaults to `⍬`. If not empt every item must be a two-item vector. The first one point to a column (first one = 0), the second one is a list. Naturally this makes sense only for "DD" & "DE".| [36] ⍝ | `lock` | Defaults to `0`. Specifies the number of columns that should **not** scroll horizontally.| [37] ⍝ | `longNumeric` | `0` | [38] ⍝ | `maxLength` | Defaults to `⍬`. If set this must be either a vector matching the number of columns or a matrix with two columns. In the latter case the first column must specify a column number. Specifies the number of characters the user may enter.| [39] ⍝ | `menu` | `0 2⍴⊂''` | ⍝TODO⍝ [40] ⍝ | `moveSelection` | The default depends on `paste`. `0` suppresses moving a selection around, `1` allows it. Instead you can specify a callback; see "Help" for details| ⍝TODO⍝ specify the help page [41] ⍝ | `moveRows` | Default to `0`. If this is `1` then the user may select one or more rows and move them up or down with Ctrl+Cursor-Up\|Down.| [42] ⍝ | `menubar` | Defaults to a standard menu; see GetDefaultMenubar for details. For no menubar set this to ''.| [43] ⍝ | `onCellChange` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must specify a user-defined callback for the "Change" event. The callback must by dyadic with `⍺=n` and `⍵` the standard message in case of a "Change" event'. Must return a Boolean: suppress=`0`, let pass=`1`.| [44] ⍝ | `onCellChanged` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must specify a user-defined callback for the "Changed" event. The callback must by monadic with `⍵=n`. Must return a result which will be ignored.| [45] ⍝ | `onClose` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must specify a user-defined callback for the "Close" event.| [46] ⍝ | `onDblClick` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must specify a user-defined callback for the "DblCLick" event. Must except a right argument (`n`) and return a result that will be ignored.| [47] ⍝ | `onKeyPress` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must specify a user-defined callback for the "KeyPress" event. Such a callback is fed with `⍺=n` and the standard KeyPress message as `⍵`. Must return either a Boolean (0=ignore) or the (possibly modified) KeyPress message.| [48] ⍝ | `onSort` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must specify a user-defined callback for the "Sort" event.| ⍝TODO⍝ : How to qualify? Argument(s)? Result? Sort what exactly? [49] ⍝ | `parmType` | Constant that is "Table" | [50] ⍝ | `paste` | Defaults to `1`. This means that the menu "Paste" item is active.| [51] ⍝ | `posn` | `⍬ ⍬` | [52] ⍝ | `readOnly` | Defaults to `0`. Set this to `1` to make the whole table read-only, or specify a vector. The length must match the number of columns then.| [53] ⍝ | `rowPrototype` | There is no default. This can become either an array or a dyadic function returning an array. A function name must be fully qualified. The result is going to be a new row added by "AutoExpand". `⍵` is the `n` namespace.| [54] ⍝ | `row_Cmds` | Defaults to `1`. Use this to disable all row-related command in one go with `0`.| [55] ⍝ | `row_Del` | Defaults to `¯1`, meaning that it depends on row_Cmds. If row_Cmds is `1` then you can still disable the "Delete row" menu item by setting this to `0`.| [56] ⍝ | `row_Duplicate` | Defaults to `¯1`, meaning that it depends on row_Cmds. If row_Cmds is `1` then you can still disable the "Duplicate row" menu item by setting this to `0`.| [57] ⍝ | `row_Insert` | Defaults to `¯1`, meaning that it depends on row_Cmds. If row_Cmds is `1` then you can still disable the "Insert row" menu item by setting this to `0.| [58] ⍝ | `rowTitleFCol` | `0` | ⍝TODO⍝ [59] ⍝ | `rowTitles` | Defaults to `''`. If specified this must be a vector of text vectors. The length must match the number of rows. Can be a vector of text vector or a simple text vector with ";" as separator.| [60] ⍝ | `rowTitleDepth` | `⍬` | ⍝TODO⍝ [61] ⍝ | `search` | Defaults to `1`. If you don''t want to offer a "Search" menu cnmmand then set this to `0`.| [62] ⍝ | `selItems` | Defaults to `⍬`. You can specify a pre-selection by setting this. However, it will be overwritten in case `settings` contains "selItems".| [63] ⍝ | `settings` | Defaults to `⍬`. This can be used to re-assign the parameters returned via the second item of `Edit`.| [64] ⍝ | `showInput` | Defaulst to `⍬`. That means that the input is shown for "Time" and "Date" but not for any other data type.| [65] ⍝ | `size` | `⍬ ⍬` | [66] ⍝ | `statusbar` | Defaults to `1`. To suppress the status bar set this to `0`.| [67] ⍝ | `testMode` | Defaults to `0`. If one then any dialogs within `Table` use the autoPress feature. | [68] ⍝ | `testParms` | Defaults to an empty matrix: `(0 2⍴'')`. Use this to specify parameters for sub-dialogs. For example, by specifying `1 2⍴('AskFor_SaveChanges' ('autoPress' 1))` you can make sure that the "Save your changes?" dialog will press the "No" button.| [69] ⍝ | `titleWidth` | `¯1` | ⍝TODO⍝ [70] ⍝ | `titleHeight` | `¯1` | ⍝TODO⍝ [71] ⍝ | `V_scroll` | Defaults to `¯3`. That means that there is always a vertical scroll bar.| [72] ⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝⍝ →[73] p←##.APLGuiParameterSpace.Create →[74] p.askNewRowTitle←¯1 →[75] p.autoExpand←1 →[76] p.addToCaption←¯1 →[77] p.caption←'' →[78] p.cellSelect←'' →[79] p.cellWidths←⍬ →[80] p.centerIn←'' →[81] p.copy←1 →[82] p.ctrl_Pos1_End←'LikeExcel' →[83] p.colTitleFCol←0 →[84] p.colTitles←'' →[85] p.colTitleDepth←⍬ →[86] p.comments←'' →[87] p.comment_Cmds←0 →[88] p.comment_Add←¯1 →[89] p.comment_Edit←¯1 →[90] p.comment_delete←¯1 →[91] p.confirmQuit←1 →[92] p.contextMenu←0 2⍴⊂'' →[93] p.curCell←0 0 →[94] p.cut←1 →[95] p.data←⍬ →[96] p.dataTypes←'' →[97] p.debug←0 →[98] p.delete←1 →[99] p.editMenu←1 →[100] p.lock←0 →[101] p.help←'' →[102] p.H_scroll←¯3 →[103] p.icon←'' →[104] p.inactiveColor←230 230 230 →[105] p.index←0 0 →[106] p.lists←⍬ →[107] p.longNumeric←0 →[108] p.maxLength←⍬ →[109] p.menu←0 2⍴⊂'' →[110] p.moveSelection←¯1 →[111] p.moveRows←0 →[112] p.menubar←¯1 →[113] p.onCellChange←'' →[114] p.onCellChanged←'' →[115] p.onClose←'' →[116] p.onDblClick←'' →[117] p.onKeyPress←'' →[118] p.onSort←'' →[119] p.parmType←'Table' →[120] p.paste←1 →[121] p.posn←⍬ ⍬ →[122] p.readOnly←0 →[123] p.row_Cmds←1 →[124] p.row_Del←¯1 →[125] p.row_Duplicate←¯1 →[126] p.row_Insert←¯1 →[127] p.rowPrototype←'' →[128] p.rowTitleFCol←0 →[129] p.rowTitles←'' →[130] p.rowTitleDepth←⍬ →[131] p.search←1 →[132] p.selItems←⍬ →[133] p.settings←⍬ →[134] p.showInput←⍬ →[135] p.size←⍬ ⍬ →[136] p.statusbar←1 →[137] p.testMode←0 →[138] p.testParms←0 2⍴'' →[139] p.titleWidth←¯1 →[140] p.titleHeight←¯1 →[141] p.V_scroll←¯3

#.APLGUI.APLGUI.TableEdit.ConvertDateBack — 0%↟↑

#.APLGUI.APLGUI.TableEdit.UnifyDate — 0%↟↑
r←UnifyDate date [1] ⍝ Convert all supported DA formats to IDF (International Date Format, '2000-10-01') →[2] :If 0=≢date~' ' →[3] r←'' →[4] :ElseIf 645=⎕DR date →[5] r←,Date2IDF 10000 100 100⊤⌊date ⍝ Is something like yyyymmdd.hhmmss →[6] :ElseIf ' '=1↑0⍴↑date →[7] 'Invalid date'⎕SIGNAL 11/⍨10≠≢date →[8] 'Invalid date'⎕SIGNAL 11/⍨8≠≢date~'-' →[9] 'Invalid date'⎕SIGNAL 11/⍨'--'≢date~⎕D →[10] r←date ⍝ Is already IDF →[11] :ElseIf (⎕DR date)∊83 163 323 ⍝ Is it an integer? →[12] :If 1=≢date ⍝ Scalar or one-item vector? →[13] :If (date>0)∧(date<2958465) ⍝ Is it an IDN? →[14] r←,Date2IDF 3↑2 ⎕NQ #'IDNToDate'date →[15] :ElseIf 0>date →[16] 'Invalid date'⎕SIGNAL 11 [17] :Else →[18] r←,Date2IDF 10000 100 100⊤date [19] :EndIf →[20] :ElseIf (≢date)∊3 6 7 →[21] r←,Date2IDF 3↑date ⍝ A timestamp [22] :Else →[23] 'Invalid date'⎕SIGNAL 11 [24] :EndIf [25] :Else →[26] 'Invalid date'⎕SIGNAL 11 [27] :EndIf

#.APLGUI.APLGUI.TableEdit.CompareWith — 0%↟↑
R←X CompareWith Y [1] ⍝⍝ X sind "alte" Values [2] ⍝⍝ Y sind "neue" Values [3] ⍝⍝ Da die Sortierreihenfolge sich geändert haben kann, reicht ein ≡ nicht aus! [4] ⍝⍝ R wird 1 wenn sich etwas geändert hat. →[5] :If X≡Y →[6] R←0 [7] :Else →[8] :If ≢/↑∘⍴¨X Y →[9] R←1 ⍝ Länge stimmt nicht, also... [10] :Else →[11] R←0∊(⊂[1]X)∊⊂[1]Y [12] :EndIf [13] :EndIf

#.APLGUI.APLGUI.TableViewer.CreateParms — 0%↟↑
r←CreateParms [1] :Access Public Shared [2] ⍝ Returns a namespace with default values for creating a form via a call to `Table.CreateGrid`. [3] ⍝ Note that all other (more specialized) `CreateParmsFor*` funtions call this one first, then add _ [4] ⍝ add new or modify existing ones. [5] ⍝ || ''Parameter name'' ||''Comment''|| [6] ⍝ || `addToCaption` || Together with the "caption" this is used to compile the caption.|| [7] ⍝ || `cellWidths` || Defaults to ⍬. Can be scalar or a vector instead. Vector length must match number of columns in table.|| [8] ⍝ || `colTitles` || Defaults to `''`. Can be scalar or a vector instead. Vector length must match number of columns in table.|| [9] ⍝ || `datatypes` || Defaults to ⍬. Can be scalar or a vector instead. Vector length must match number of columns in table. See `Enums.TableDataTypes`. || [10] ⍝ || `decimals` || Defaults to 0. Can be scalar or a vector instead. Vector length must match number of columns in table.|| [11] ⍝ || `fontInput` || Font to be used inside the table.|| [12] ⍝ || `modalWait` || Boolean that defaults to 1. A 1 will make `Browse` dequeue the Form. Useful for adding a menubar etc.|| [13] ⍝ || `size` || Defaults to `(⍬ ⍬)`. Set this to a two-item vector in case you want the Form to have a certain size.|| [14] ⍝ || `parent` || Form or SubForm hosting the grid. Defaults to n.∆Form if not specified.|| [15] ⍝ || `parmsType` || Must be "BrowseTable".|| [16] ⍝ || `posn` || Defaults to `(⍬ ⍬)`. Use this to specify a certain position for the Form.|| [17] ⍝ || `rowTitles` || Defaults to rows numbered from 1. Use 0 to suppress row titles. Otherwise it must be a vector of text vectors matching the table cols.|| →[18] r←##.APLGuiParameterSpace.Create →[19] r.addToCaption←'' →[20] r.cellWidths←⍬ →[21] r.colTitles←'' →[22] r.dataTypes←⍬ →[23] r.decimals←⍬ →[24] r.fontInput←¯1 →[25] r.modalWait←1 →[26] r.menu←GetBrowseMenu ⍬ →[27] r.size←⍬ ⍬ →[28] r.parent←¯1 →[29] r.parmType←'BrowseTable' →[30] r.posn←⍬ ⍬ →[31] r.rowTitles←¯1 [32] ⍝Done

#.APLGUI.APLGUI.SelAndProcess.CalcNeededSize — 94%↟↑
size←{maxPercentage}CalcNeededSize n [1] ⍝ Calculates the size of the form. [2] ⍝ Takes "size" and "minSize" into account but also restricts the [3] ⍝ size to "maxPercentage" if that is defined. "maxPercentage" trumps [4] ⍝ "size" while "minSize" trumps "size" only when greater. [5] ⍝ Normally "maxPercentage" is specified only at the first call after [6] ⍝ a form got created. Purpose is to avoid monster forms to be [7] ⍝ created on very large monitors. Later the user might actually ask [8] ⍝ for a monster form, so we allow this then. [9] fac←1+≢n.List.Items [10] lineHeight←GetListViewItemHeight n.List [11] height←5+n.List.Posn[0]+fac×lineHeight [12] height+←(4+lineHeight)×0≠≢n.∆parms.colTitles [13] :If 0=≢n.List.ReportInfo [14] width←⌈/1⊃¨n.List.GetTextSize¨n.List.Items [15] :Else [16] buf←1⊃n.List.GetTextSize(4+⌈/≢¨n.List.Items)⍴'W' [17] buf+←1⊃n.List.GetTextSize({+/(2+≢⍵)+⍵}↑¨⌈⌿≢¨n.List.ReportInfo)⍴'W' [18] width←buf [19] :EndIf [20] width⌈←n.∆Hgap+1⊃↑+/↑¯1↑n.∆buttons.(Posn Size) ⍝ Take right-most button into account [21] width⌈←n.Info.Size[1]+2×n.∆Hgap ⍝ Take the "Info" field into account [22] corr←##.WinSys.(GetSystemMetrics SM_CYCAPTION) [23] corr+←##.WinSys.(GetSystemMetrics SM_CYMENU) [24] height+←corr [25] height+←n.Btn_00.Size[0] [26] width+←30×n.List.CheckBoxes [27] fntObj←⎕NEW'Font'((⊂¨'PName' 'Weight' 'Size'),¨⊂∘{(' '=1↑0⍴⍵):⍵~⎕UCS 0 ⋄ |⍵}¨##.WinSys.GetFormCaptionFontInfo[13 4 0]) [28] btns←##.WinSys.(GetSystemMetrics SM_CXICON)×1+n.∆Form.(SysMenu+MaxButton+MinButton) [29] width⌈←btns+1⊃n.∆Form.GetTextSize n.∆Form.Caption fntObj [30] width⌈←##.APLGuiUtils.CalcSizeOfFormCaption n [31] available←##.APLGuiHelpers.MonitorEstate [32] :If height>available[0] [33] height←available[0] ⍝ But not more than the size of the screen [34] :EndIf [35] :If width>available[1] →[36] width←available[1] ⍝ But not more than the size of the screen →[37] height+←2+##.WinSys.(GetSystemMetrics SM_CXVSCROLL) [38] :EndIf [39] size←height,width [40] :If 0<⎕NC'maxPercentage' [41] size⌊←⌊(0.01×maxPercentage)×##.APLGuiHelpers.ScreenEstate-corr [42] :EndIf [43] size←n.∆parms.size{(0=≢⍺):⍵ ⋄ ⍺}¨size [44] size←n.∆parms.minSize{(0=≢⍺):⍵ ⋄ ⍺⌈⍵}¨size

#.APLGUI.APLGUI.OptionsTool.CreateParms — 100%↟↑
r←CreateParms [1] :Access Public Shared [2] ⍝ Returns a parameter namespace with default settings. [3] ⍝ | Parameter name | Comments | [4] ⍝ |-|-| [5] ⍝ | `addToCaption` | Together with the "caption" this is used to compile the caption| [6] ⍝ | `alignButtons` | Defaults to `##.Enums.Align.Left`. Can be "center" or "right" instead. Overwrites global parameter if not empty.| [7] ⍝ | `autoPress` | Defines the button that is clicked in test mode (`0`=OK, `1`=Cancel, `3`=Help, `¯1`=no button at all).| [8] ⍝ | `caption` | This overwrites any setting of "caption" in the global parameter set.| [9] ⍝ | `centerIn` | A form the new form shall be centered in.| [10] ⍝ | `default` | Which items shall be preselected from the start. Must be Boolean.| [11] ⍝ | `destroyAutoPressTimerHotKey` | Which key allows the user to stop the timer. Defaults to F12| [12] ⍝ | `fontAPL` | Font to be used for displaying APL code.| [13] ⍝ | `fontInput` | Font to be used for any input field.| [14] ⍝ | `fontLabel` | Font to be used for lables.| [15] ⍝ | `fontStandard` | Font to be used for any other purposes than "fontAPL", "fontInput" and "fontLabel".| [16] ⍝ | `hasStatusbar` | Boolean that decides whether the form will have a status bar or not. Defaults to 1.| [17] ⍝ | `hasVersionField` | Reserve a field in the bottom right corner for version info.| [18] ⍝ | `hGap` | Horizontal gap. Defaults to the value of `hGap` in the global parameter space.| [19] ⍝ | `icon` | Default is an empty vector. Might become either an icon object or a `*.ico` filename. The latter one must be prefixed by "file:///".| [20] ⍝ | `info` | If specified this string is shown above the list.| [21] ⍝ | `initialVgap` | The space between the top border of the Form and the first control. Defaults to 3 times the vGap.| [22] ⍝ | `minSize` | Defaults to 200 300. Use this to specify the minimum size of the resulting Form. If controls demand a bigger size anyway, `minSize` has no effect.| [23] ⍝ | `parent` | If specified this must point to a form that will be the parent of the dialog to be created.| [24] ⍝ | `parmType` | Is "SelectionTool"; defines the type of this parameter space.| [25] ⍝ | `posn` | Posn of the form.| [26] ⍝ | `regKey2` | If this is non-empty it it used to save "Posn" & "Size" for the Form. [3] ⍝ | Parameter name | Comments | [4] ⍝ |-|-| [5] ⍝ | `addToCaption` | Together with the "caption" this is used to compile the caption| [6] ⍝ | `alignButtons` | Defaults to `##.Enums.Align.Left`. The latter one must be prefixed by "file:///".| [23] ⍝ | `info` | If specified this string is shown above the list.| [24] ⍝ | `initialVgap` | The space between the top border of the Form and the first control. Defaults to 3 times the vGap.| [25] ⍝ | `minSize` | Defaults to 200 300. Use this to specify the minimum size of the resulting Form. If controls demand a bigger size anyway, `minSize` has no effect.| [26] ⍝ | `okBtnCaption` | Caption of the "OK" (default) button.| [27] ⍝ | `parent` | If specified this must point to a form that will be the parent of the dialog to be created.| [28] ⍝ | `parmType` | Is "SelectionTool"; defines the type of this parameter space.| [29] ⍝ | `posn` | Posn of the form.| [30] ⍝ | `regKey2` | If this is non-empty it it used to save "Posn" & "Size" for the Form. Set to `¯1` if `regKey2` should become `addToCaption`. `''` means: don't save (default).| [31] ⍝ | `savePosnAndSizeInRegistry` | Boolean that defaults to 1: save "Posn" & "Size" in the Windows Registry.| [32] ⍝ | `size` | Size of the form.| [33] ⍝ | `sortByCol` | Allow (1) or disallow (0) sorting by a click on a/the col title.| [34] ⍝ | `statusbarMessage` | If you want an initial message in the status bar set this.| [35] ⍝ | `style` | How many rows may the user select: "single" or "multi". Set to "multi" in case checkboxes=1.| [36] ⍝ | `testMode` | Defaults to `¯1`. This will become `globals.testmode` if its not overwritten.| [37] ⍝ | `timeout` | Boolean that defaults to 0. 1 means that the "autoPress" button is "pressed" after "timeout" seconds.| [38] ⍝ | `useWindowsRegistry` | If this is one then "Posn" & "Size" are saved/loaded in/from the Windows Registry.| [39] ⍝ | `vGap` | Vertical gap.
